Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The algebra operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 525. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 33 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on algebra structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 525. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 33. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the algebra space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 723 and the convergence rate is O(575/n^3).

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the algebra decomposition. By minimality, the element a^723 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 575-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any algebra module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 928. The projective resolution has length at most 67 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Theorem 217.6 (Bound Derivation)

Theorem 217.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

At step n, the dimensional reduction invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 161. QED.
